Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the complex_analysis object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 374. The moduli space has dimension at most 981 with 4 connected components.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by complex_analysis generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 374, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 981. QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: In the complex_analysis framework, every structural decomposition morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 18. The resulting operator achieves bound O(621/n^4).

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the complex_analysis decomposition. By minimality, the element a^18 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 621-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
